## Self-review notes

Checked against `2026-09-02-markdown-domain-design.md`, section by
section:

- **§1 What this sub-project is** — versioned prose attached to game
  content, with the five "deliberately not" boundaries held: no
  wikilinks or backlink index (there is no link syntax anywhere in Tasks
  2 or 11), no draft/publish states (Task 4's only state is
  `deleted_at`), no instance-global scope (`project_id NOT NULL`, Task
  1), no branching-dialogue structure (nothing in this plan reads inside
  a body except the search vector and the title derivation), and
  frontmatter that is stored and never interpreted (Task 2, pinned by
  `TestFrontmatterIsSplitOffAndTheBodyIsWhatWasWrittenAfterIt` and by
  Task 12's step 13).
- **§2 Attachment** — documents as a first-class table keyed by
  `(project_id, lower(path))`, with a many-to-many `document_links`:
  Task 1. Zero entities and two entities are both exercised by content
  in Task 12's fixture. The composite keys the section's own reconciled
  note demands are Task 1's, pinned by
  `TestADocumentLinkCannotCrossGames` and
  `TestADocumentVersionCannotCrossGames`.
- **§3 Versioning** — full snapshots not diffs, the version record with
  its two nullable author columns, versions never pruned (no tool can),
  soft delete with resurrection and continued numbering, revert writing
  forward: Tasks 3, 4 and 6. The two divergences from Nottario the
  section names — no `scope`, no fixed `kind` enum — are Tasks 1 and 3.
- **§4 Concurrent editing** — `expected_version` as a compare-and-set in
  SQL under a `FOR UPDATE` read, `version_conflict` carrying the current
  version *and body* under `include_current`: Tasks 3 and 10. The four
  things deliberately not done — OT, CRDT, locking, server-side merge —
  are absent, and section-scoped writes stay open, recorded in this
  plan's "Open questions settled".
- **§5 Isolation** — every query filtered on the resolved project id
  (Tasks 3, 6, 7, 8, 9), `document_versions.project_id` denormalised and
  never reached through a join (Task 6's queries say so in their own
  comments), the composite keys that make a cross-game row impossible
  (Task 1), and the mandatory sweep of every tool with a foreign game's
  token including `read_version`, `history` and `links.add` (Task 12,
  step 12).
- **§6 Data model** — every column of the section's DDL, with the
  foreign keys corrected to composite as the section itself demands, the
  generated weighted `tsvector`, `current_version` as the concurrency
  token, and title/summary derived rather than authored twice: Tasks 1
  and 2.
- **§7 MCP and REST surface** — the eleven `docs.*` tools with the
  section's exact arguments (Task 10), `expected_version: 0` as a
  create, `links` replacing versus omitting, attachment never inferred,
  entity-filtered listing (Tasks 7, 8, 10), bodies returned only by
  read, read_version and diff (Tasks 3, 6, 8), the REST mirror with the
  rendered reading view and the comparison (Task 11), and the four
  `/events` kinds (Tasks 3, 4, 6, 7, received end to end in Task 11).
  The section's two proposed error codes are **deliberately not
  implemented**, and this plan's "Open questions settled" argues each
  refusal.
- **§8 Search** — a second generated index weighted title > summary >
  body (Task 1), one surface returning labelled hits of both kinds with
  a kind filter (Task 9), entity search that does not reach into
  attached prose and document hits that name their entities (Task 9,
  asserted in Task 12's step 10), and only current versions indexed
  (Task 9). The section's open question about the text-search
  configuration is **settled** — `simple` on both sides — and argued in
  "Open questions settled".
- **§9 Testing and definition of done** — its five mandatory areas map
  to Tasks 3–9's own suites and are re-run as content in Task 12: game
  isolation (step 12), concurrency (steps 2–3), version integrity
  (steps 6–8), links (steps 1, 4, 11), search (step 10). The definition
  of done itself is Task 12, end to end, plus its hand-verification
  against a running instance.
- **§10 Open questions** — all six are addressed in "Open questions
  settled": section-scoped writes and relation attachment and export are
  explicitly out of scope, the text-search configuration and the two
  error codes are decided, `role` stays free text with its key
  consequence stated, and `kind` ships bounded.

Deliberately **not** here, and correctly so: the view query language and
its renderers (views sub-project), prerequisite-cycle and
unreachable-content detection (analysis sub-project), the agent skill
bundle that teaches an agent when to write a document rather than a
field, the visual identity work the `impeccable` skill owns, and export
of a game's prose as a folder of `.md` files, which the spec flags as
belonging with archiving and backups.

Two things this plan changes that live **outside** the markdown domain,
named here so a reviewer looks at them first:

1. **`internal/paging`** (Task 5) moves shipped, reviewed code out of
   `internal/metamodel`. Its acceptance test is that no metamodel test
   changes, and Task 5's Step 7 re-mutates the seam to confirm the old
   tests still reach through it.
2. **The `search` tool's output shape** (Task 9) changes for existing
   callers. Four test files assert the old shape and all four change in
   the same commit as the code.
